विषयः सर्वश्री स्यूजाहिक ट्यूब इन्जीनियस, 28, गाला इन्धस्ट्रिीयल एस्टेट, उम्पिग रोड, मुलन्द , बम्बई- 80 को 35,142 रुपए के लिए जारी किए गए 

लाईसेंस संख्या 2728085, दिनांक 10- 2-76 की सीमा मुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति को रदव करने का मादेश । 


मा० मा0 4008. -- सर्वभी, स्यूनाडिज ट्युब इन्जीमियर्स , बम्बई- 80 को ए 60- 2 ( ए ) और ए 66- 2 ( ख ) के अनर्गत निर्यात के मद मनुमेय प्रकार के सोम - 
संस कार्बन स्टील ट्यूम्म और कोड डाल के पायात के लिए 35, 142 रुपए का उपर्युक्न लाईसेस प्रदत्त किया गया था । अब उम्हाने उपयुक्त माईसेंस की 
सीमा शुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति की अनुलिपि प्रति के लिए इस प्राधार पर प्रावेदन किया है कि उपर्युक्त लाइमेस का भून सीमा शुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति सीमा शुल्क 
कार्यालय , बम्बई के पास पंजीकृत कराने के पश्चात पौर प्राशिक रूप से उपयोग में लाने के पाचात् खो गई / अम्यानस्थ हो गई है । कुल धनराशि 
जिसके लिए लाईमेंस जारी किया गया था वह 35,142 रुपए है और कुल धनराशि जिसके लिए शोध राशि को पूरा करने के लिए अनुलिपि प्रति की प्राप 
एमकता है वह 8, 312 रुपए है । 


इम तर्क के ममर्थन मे , अावेदा ने पंजीयक और महानगरीय जिलाधीण, बम्बई के मम्मन्न माग कागज पर विधिवत् माझ्याकिन एक शपथपत्र दाखिल किया है ।

: 


मैं संतुष्ट हूं कि साईसेंस संख्या 2728065, दिनांक 10- 2- 76 की मुल सीमा शुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति बो गई पथबा प्रस्थानस्म हो गई । पौर निषेश घेता 
है कि प्रावेदक को उपर्युक्त लाईसेंम की अनुलिपि सीमा शुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति जारी की जाए । मूल सीमा शुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति 2728085 दिनांक 10- 2- 76 
रर की जाती है । 
विषयः - साईसेंस संख्या 2728085, दिनांक 10- 2-76 की सीमा शुल्क प्रयोजन प्रनि को अनुलिपि प्रति जारी करना । 

प्रापको यह सूचित किया जाता है कि सर्वश्री स्यूबा डिज ट्यूब इन्जीनियर्स , बम्बई- 80 को लाईसेंस सख्या -पी / यू 2728085, दिनांक 10- 2 - 76 को 
प्रलिपि सीमा शुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति जारी की गई है । यह अनुरोध किया जाता है कि मूल लाईसेंस (जिसका पीरा नीचे दिया गया है ) प्रस्तुत करने पर वैष 
म समझा जाए और यदि उपर्युक्त लाईसेंस की मूल प्रति पहले ही अपने पत्तन पर प्रस्तुत कर दी गई है अथवा उसका उपयोग कर लिया गया है तो उसकी 
सूचना तत्काल ही इस कार्यालय को भेजी जाए । 

लाईसेस फा म्यारा निम्न प्रकार है

___ M /s. Lu Bra Diz Tube Engineers , 28 , Gula Industrial Estate, Dumping Road , Mulund , Bombay 80 . 


s . o . 4006. --- M / s Lu Bra Diz Tube Enginoors , Bombay 80 were granted Import Licence mentioned abovo for Rs. 35142 for import of 
Soamless Carbon Steel Tubes and Cold Drawn permissible sizes against exports undor A . 66 - 2 ( A ) and A. 66- 2 ( B ), They have now applied 
for duplicate copy of Customs purposo copy of the abovo liconce on the ground that the original customspurpose copy of the above lloenco 
has bocn lost/misplaced after having boop registered with tho CustomsHouse , Bombay and utilised partially . The total amount for which 
the licence was issued is Rs. 35142 and the total amount for which the duplicato required is to cover the balance of Rs. 8312 . 


In support of this contention the applicants have filed an affidavit on stamped paper duly attested before the Registrar and Metropo . 
litan Magistrato , Bombay , 


I am satisfied that the original Customs purpose copy of licence No . 2728085 dated 10 -2 - 76 has been lost or misplaced and direct that 
duplicate customs purpose copy of the above licence should be issued to the applicant. The original customs purpose copy 2728085 dated 
10- 2 - 1976 is cancelled .

का०मा० 4011. - -जीरा श्रेगोकरण और चिह्नांकन नियम , 1969 
में संशोधन करने के लिए नियमों का एक प्रारूप , कृषि उपज ( धेणीकरण 
और चिह्नांकन ) अधिनियम , 1937 ( 1937 का 1 ) की धारा 3 
द्वारा यया अपेक्षित न में , भारत सरकार के कृषि और सिंचाई मंत्रालय 
( ग्रामीण विकास विभाग ) की अधिसूचना संख्या का प्रा० 3319 तारीख 
30 मितम्बर, 1977 के अधीन भारत के राजपत्र , भाग 2 , खण्ड 3 , 
उपखण्ड ( दो ) तारीख 22 अक्तूबर, 1977 के पृष्ठ 3768 पर प्रकाशित 
किया गया था , जिसमें उक्त अधिसूचना के राजपत्र में प्रकाशन की 
गरख से पेंताजीग दिनों की अवधि को समाप्ति तक उन सभी व्यक्तियों 
से आपत्तियां जोर सुझाव मागे र थे , जिनके उससे प्रनावित होने की 
सम्भावना है । 

और उन राजपत्र 22 अक्तूबर, 1977 को जनता को उपलब्ध 
करा दिया गया था ; 

और केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त प्रारूप को धाबत प्राप्त आपत्तियों 
और सुझावों पर विचार कर लिया है । 

अत., अत्र, केन्द्रीय सरकार, उस अधिनियम की धारा 3 द्वारा 
प्रान गलितयों का प्रयोग करते हुए, जोरा श्रेगोकरण और चिह्नांकन

उन सभी व्यक्तियों की जानकारी के लिए प्राशित किया जाता है जिनका

प्रारूप पर, उस तारीख से , जिसको उस राजपन की प्रतियां , जिसमें 
यह अधिसूचना प्रकाशित की जाती है, जनता को उपलब्ध कराई जाती 
है , तीस दिन को अवधि कि समाप्ति पर या उसके पश्चात् विचार किया 
जाएगा । 


MINISTRY OF RURAL RECONSTRUCTION 

New Delhi, the 30th November , 1979 
S . 0 . 4011. - Whereas the draft rules to amend the Cuminseed 
Grading and Maiking Rules , 1969 , were published as required 
by section 3 of the Agricultural Produce (Grading and 
Marking ) Act, 1937 ( 1 of 1937) , at page 3768 of the Gazette 
of India , Part II , Section 3 , Sub- section ( ii ), dated 22nd 
October, 1977 , with the notification of the Government of 
India in the Ministry of Agriculture and Irrigation (Depart 

ment of Rural Development ) No . S. O . 3319, dated 30th 
September , 1977, inviting objections and suggestions from all 
persons likely to be affected thereby within a period of fourty 
five days from the date on which the copies of the Gazette 
containing the said notification were made available to the 
public ; 

And whereas the said Gazette was made available to the 
public on the 22nd October , 1977 ; 

And whereas objections and suggestions received from the 
public on the said draft have been considered by the Central 
Government ; 

Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by 
section 3 of the said Act , the Central Goveroment hereby 
makes the following rules to amend the Cuminseed Grading 
and Marking Rules, 1969 , namely : 


उक्त प्रारूप की बाबत किसी भी व्यक्ति से इस प्रकार विनिर्दिष्ट 
अवधि की समाप्ति से पूर्व प्राप्त किसो भी प्रापत्ति या सुझाव पर केन्द्रीय 
सरकार विचार करेगी । 

नियमों का प्रारूप 
1. इन नियमों का नाम अदरक श्रेणीकरण और चिह्ननन ( संशोधन ) 
नियम , 1979 है । 

2 अदरक श्रेणीकरण और चिहन निगम , 1964 में , अनुसूची 10 
में , स्तम्भ 5 के शोषक में , अधिकतन शब्द के स्थान पर न्यूनतम 
शब्द रखा जाएगा । 

[ सं० 13 ( 9 ) - 73-ए० एन०]

tthe Central Government purpose to make , in exercise of the

fication is published are made available to the public ,

(ii ) अनुसूची 2 के बाद, निम्नलिखित अनुसूची भन्तःस्थापित की जाएगी , पर्थात :--- 

मनुसूची 25 
भारत में उत्पादित विनिर्मित फ्ल्यू क्योई वीनिया (पिटाई के बाद का पनवल ) तम्बाकू का श्रेणी अभिधान और वर्णम । 

1, पिटा हुमा पत्रदल फम्यूक्योर्ड वर्जीनिया प्रजाति की और उसकी संकर किस्मों की जिनके अभिलक्षण एक जैसे है , श्रेणीकृत तम्बाकू को पीटकर प्राप्त 
किया जाता है और इसमें पसों के ऐसे सिरे, जो पसे की लगभग 1/ 3 लम्बाई को काटकर प्राप्त होते है और सिरा -कटी पत्ती ( बचा हुमा भाग ) की मंत्र 
ारा पीटकर प्राप्त भिन्न -भिन्न लम्बाइयों के प्रोकिन विट्स या टुकड़े है । 

2. पिटे हुए पत्रपल में , पत्तों के सिरे और सिरे कटे पत्तों से प्राप्त विभिन्न लम्बाई के ऐसे घोफिन विट्स या दुकड़े है, जो यंत्रों में तोड़े गए है , किन्तु 
उसकी निम्नलिखित शत है :- - 

( क ) पते के सिरों का प्राकार लम्बाई में 3 से 6 के बीच होगा । 
( ख ) सिर कटे पत्तों से प्राप्त प्रोफिन बिट्स या टुकड़ों का प्राकार लम्बाई में 1/ 4 " से 6 " के बीच होगा । 
( ग ) पत्तों के सिरों का भार कुल पत्रवल के भार के 25 प्रतिशत से अधिक नहीं होगा । 
( प ) पिटे हुए पलवल में 1/ 2" से अधिक लम्बे टुकड़े कुल उत्पाद के कम से कम 70 प्रतिशत ( भार के अनुसार ) होंगे । 
( 6 ) पटे हए पन्नवल में 1/ 4 के प्रकार से कम के टुकड़े कुल उत्पाद के 10 प्रतिशत ( भार के प्रमुसार ) से अधिक नहीं होंगे । 
( ) पिटेदए पनवल में , स्टेम का कुल भार कुल उत्पाद के 5 प्रतिशत से अधिक नहीं होगा । 
३. पिटे हुए पनवल का निर्यात और पैकिंग निम्नलिखित शास्तों पर होगी : 
( क ) पिटे हुए पत्नवल के लिए नियत की जाने वाली श्रेणी, पिटाई के लिए पूर्ण पत्ते भेजने से पहले अवधारित की जाएगो । अनुसषी 2 में बताए 

गए विनिर्मित फुलू क्योर्ड वर्जीनिया सम्बाकू को लागू होने वाला श्रेणी विनिर्देश , तम्बाकू की पिटाई संयंत्र में डालने से पहले , प्रवर्तित किया जाएगा । 
( ब ) किसी विशिष्ट श्रेणी के लिए मिरीक्षित और अनुमोदित तम्बाकू की ही निरीक्षण अधिकारी की उपस्थिति में , पिटाई करने की अनुमति दी जाएगी 

पौर एगमार्क लेबल पर " पिटा हुमा पशदल " पर फोष्ठक में सथा श्रेणी अभिधान चिहन के साथ अंकित किया जाएगा । 
( ग ) एगमार्क बिहनांकित प्रविनिर्मित फ्ल्यू क्योर वर्जीनिया तम्बाकू के पैकेजों की भांति ही , श्रेणीकृत "पिटा हमा पबवल " तम्बाकू के पैकेजों के भी 

प्रायिक जाँच नमूने लिए जाएंगे और जाब निरीक्षण किया जाएगा । 
(ii) अनुसूची III के पश्चात् निम्नलिखित अनुसूची अन्तःस्थापित की जाएगी , प्रात् :---

का प्रयोग करते हुए काली मिर्च ( गीकरण और चिन्हातान नियन , 1960 से पति और संगोपन करता बाहो है । जैगा कि उक्त धारा में अपेक्षित है , 
प्रस्तावित संशोधनों का निम्नलिजित प्रारूप उन सभी प्रतिया को जानकारी के लिए TT का नाम है, जिनके उस मनि हाने की सम्भावना 
है । इसके द्वारा सूचना दी जाती है कि उक्त प्राम पर इस अग्नि है राजा से मकान की तारीख में तालिम दिन के पश्चात् विचार किया जाएगा ।

करेगी ।

SO 4014 . The following draft of certain rules to amend the Pepper Grading and Marking Rules, 1969 , which the Central Govornment 

ato make in exercise of the powers conferred by section 3 of the Agricultural Produce (Grading and Marking ) Act , 1937 (1 of 1937) , 
Sreby published , as required by the said section , for the information of all persons likely to be affected thereby and notice is hereby given 
that the said draft will be taken into consideration on or after the expiry of fortyfive days from the date on which the Official Gazetto contain 
ing this notification is made available to the public . 

Any objections or suggestions which may be received from any persons with respect to the said draft before the date so specified will 
be considered by the Central Government. 

DRAFT RULES : 
1. (i) These rules may be called the Pepper Grading and Marking ( Amendment) Rules , 1979. 

2 . (1) In the Pepper Grading and Marking Rules, 1969, (herein after referred to as tho said rules ) for rule 4 , the following rule shall bo 
substituted, namely : 

" 4 . Definition of quality . The quality indicated by the rospective grado designation shall be as set out against each grado desire

से सम्बन्धित किसी सम्पदा अधिकारी के समक्ष लम्बित किन्ही मामलों

के स्तम्भ 1 में उस्लिखित राजपत्रित अधिकारी को प्राधिकार भारती है ।

It has becomo necessary to give retrospective effect to the 
exemption in this case , as the rocommendation of the ESI 
Corporation for grant of exemption was recoivcd lats How 
ever, it is certified that grant of exemption with retrospective

मा० पा० 4020 - केन्द्रीय सरकार, कर्मचारी , राज्य बीमा 
अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948 का 34 ) की धारा 912 के माय पठित 
भारा 87 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करसे हुए, तथा भारत सरकार 
के श्रम मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना संख्या का० मा0 2029, दिनांक 29 अन , 
1978 के मनुक्रम में , हिन्दुस्तान एयरोनाटिक्स लिमिटेड , कानपुर को उक्त 
अधिनियम के प्रवर्तन से 1 जुलाई , 1978 से 31 मार्च, 1979 तक, जिसमें 
यह विन भी सम्मिलित है , की और अवधि के लिए छूट देती है । 

2. पूर्णोक्त छूट की झमें निम्नलिखित हैं , पर्थात् : -- 

( 1 ) उक्त कारखाने का नियोजक, उस अवधि की पाबत जिसके 
दौरान उस कारखाने पर उक्त प्रधि नियम प्रवर्तमान था (जिसे इसमें इमले 
प्रश्चात उक्त प्रयषि कहा गया है ) , ऐसी विवरणियां, ऐसे प्ररूप में और 
ऐसी विशिष्टियों सहित देगा जो फर्मचारी राज्य बीमा ( साधारण ) 
विनियम , 1950 के प्रधीन उसे उक्त अवधि की बाबत देनी थी ; 

( 2 ) निगम द्वारा उक्त प्रधिनियम की धारा 45 की उपधारा ( 1 ) 
के प्रधान नियुक्त किया गया कोई निरीक्षक , या निगम का इस निमित्त 
प्राधिकृत कोई अन्य पदधारी --- 
( 1 ) धारा 44 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधोम , उक्त अवधि को बापत 

पो गई किसी विवरणी की विशिष्टियों को मत्यापित करने के 

प्रयोजनार्थ; या । 
( 2 ) यह अभिनिश्चित करने के प्रयोजनार्य कि कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा 

( साधारण ) विनियम , 1950 द्वारा यथाप्रपेक्षित रजिस्टर 
पौर भभिलेख, उक्त अवधि के लिए रखे गए थे या नहीं ; 


निम्नलिखित कार्य करने के लिए सशक्त होगा : 
( फ ) प्रधान या मध्यवहित नियोजक से अपेक्षा करना कि वह उमे 

ऐसी जानकारी दे जिसे उपरोक्त निरीक्षक या अन्य पदधारी 

पावश्यफ समानता है; या 
( ब ) ऐसे प्रधान या भष्यवहित नियोजक के भधिमोगाधीन किसी 

कारखाने, स्थापन, कार्यालय या अन्य परिसर में किसी भी उधिन 
समय पर प्रवेश करना और उसके प्रभारी से यह प्रपेक्षा करना 
फि वह व्यक्तियों के नियोजन भौर मजदूरी के संवाय से 
मयंधित ऐसे लेखा, पहियों और अन्य दास्तापेष , ऐसे निरीक्षक 
या मन्य पदधारी के समक्ष प्रस्तुत करें और उनकी परीक्षा 
करने थे , या उन्हें ऐसी जानकारी दे जिसे वे पावश्यक समझते 

हैं ; या 
( ग ) प्रधान या अव्यवहित नियोजन की , उसके अभिकर्ता या सेवक 

की . या ऐसे फिमी व्यक्ति की जो ऐसे कारखाने , स्थापन , 
कार्यालय या अन्य परिसर में पाया जाए, या ऐसे किसी 
न्यक्ति की जिसके बारे में उक्त निरीक्षक या अन्य पदधारी 
के पास यह विश्वास करने का युक्तियुक्त कारण है कि यह 

कर्मचारी है , परीक्षा करना ; या 
( ध ) ऐसे कारखाने , स्थापन, कार्यालय या अन्य परिसर में रखे गए 

किसी रजिस्टर , सेनापही या प्रम्य पस्तावेज की नकल तैयार 
करना या उमसे उद्धरण मेना । 

म्याध्यात्मक ज्ञापन 
इस मामले में पूर्वापेक्षा प्रभाव से छूट देना प्रावश्यक हो गया है , 
क्योंकि छूट के लिए प्राप्त भावेदन-पक्ष की कार्रवाई पर समय सगा । यह 
भी प्रमाणित किया जामा है कि पूर्वापेक्षी प्रभाष से छूट देने से किसी के 
हित पर प्रतिकूल प्रभाव नहीं पड़ेगा ।

"Whether the employeos of Dalmia Magnesito Corpora

if so what should be their Sunday wages ". 
2 . Three Uniong ospouse the cause of the workmen and 
had Aled three separate claim statement in February , March , 
and April , 1971. The Management of Ma. Dalmia Magno 
site Corporation Salem filed their counter statement on 
24 - 4 - 1971, The workmen and management placed oral and 
documentary evidence on all the materials. My learnod pre 
docessor Thirul K . Scetharama Rao rendered an award on 
30 - 1 - 1971, to the effect that the attendance bonus scheme 
has now prevalent in Burn and Company Limited will be 
given to the worker in this company from the date when this 
award is published in the Gazette and that tho payment to be 
tnade i 28 per the attendance bonus scheme owards the rest 
day wagog. The Respondent Management challenged the 
Award of this Tribunal by filing writ petition No . 476 of 1972 
on the filo of the High Court, Madras on 6 - 1 - 76 . The High 
Court dismissed tho said writ petition confirming the award 
passed by this Tribunal. Not satisfied with this Order of the 
High Court , the Management filed writ appeal No . 80 of 
1976 on the file of the High Court , Madras. That appeal was 
decexor Thiru K . Seetharama Rao rendered an award on 
6 - 7 - 1978. Eventually the Division Bench allowed the appeal 
And set aside the order passed in writ potition No . 476 of 
1972 and quashed the award of this Tribunal dated 30 - 10 - 1971 
and directed this Tribunal to reconsider the entire matter 
afresh with reference to the actual disputo referred to it . 


3 . The only point that has to be considered is whether 
the omployees of Dalmia Magnesite Corporation . Salem are 
entitled to the payment for the rest day and if so what should 
be their Sunday wages . It is common ground that the Res 
pondent Management is carrying on the business of mining 
of Magnesite and manufacture of Dead Burn Magnesite em 
ploying about 800 workers directly who are covered by tho 
present reference besides sont independent contractors and 
other staff . The workmen worked for six days in a week 
and Sunday is a day off weekly rest for them , Although the 
three unions representing the workmon employed by the Res 
pondent Company had filed separate claim statements , all of 
them are united in this namely requesting that an award may 
bo passed for mining wages for rest day Damely Sunday at 
a rate of usual current normal rates for a working day. The 
Defence put forward by the management can be gathered 
from paragraph 8 of the claim statement Aled by the Res 
pondent Management. 


4 . Thoir contentions are two -fold . In the fist place the 
daily rate of wages already given to the workmen duly take 
caro of the wages for the weekly day off rest. Secondly , as 
the provision of the minimum Wages Act have been extended 
to the Magnesite industry in case the existing rates of wages , 
were found to fall short of the minimum rates of the wages , 
the game will be raised and that therefore , there was no 
justification to press the demand for payment of wages for 
the weekly day off rest,

( In 1961 II LLJ the Supreme Court had stated as follows : 
"We express no opinion as to what the position of law is 
after this amendment of rule 23 ) . 


5. Ex . C -1 is a notification dated 1st July , 1970 of the 
Government of India adding the employment of Magnesite 
Mines to Part 1 of the Schedule to the minimum wages Act 
( Centre ) . Therefore even as per this notification the Minimui 
Wages Act had been extended to the employment in the 
Magnesite Mincs also with cffect from 1 - 7 - 1970 . That apart 
exibit M - 4 is the copy of the notification of Central Govern 
ment regarding fixation of minimum rate of wages to the 
employment in Magnesite Mines . The explanation attached 
to this notification states that the minimum rates proposed are 
all inclusive rates , including basic wages , the cost of living 
wlowances , the cash valuc of the concessions supplied if any 
value of essential commodities and also include wages payibi. 
for the weekly rest day . Therefore , it is sought to be con 
tended that when minimum wages are to be paid under tho 
Minimum Wages Act, 1948 , then as per the explanation the 
inclusive of wages payable for the weekly rest day and there 
fore the workmen will not be entitled to anything additionally 
by way of wages for weekly rest day . But, it is significant 
to note that even in the counter statement filed by the Manage 
ment on 24 - 4 - 1971 it is not stated that the workmen are 
paid wages in accordance with Minimum Wages Act of 1949 . 
The Management has also not filed any additional counter 
after demand by High Court to say that the wages paid to 
the workmen are in accordance with or above the wages 
fixed under the Minimum Wages Act, 1948 . 


6 . I shall now examine the first contention of the Manage 
ment namely that the daily rate of wages duly take care of 
thc wages for the weekly day off the rest. Exibit M - 1 is the 
award passed by this Tribunal in I .D . 39 of 1966 dated 
27 - 6 - 1968 . The dispute in those I, Ds covered the workmen 
of the Respondent Management also . Eventually in I . D . 39 
of 1966 this Tribunal fixed the rate of wages for the workers 
of Respondent Management. The Tribunal fixed the monthly 
wages of the worker on the following lines : 


" To arrive at monthly wages , the above daily wages 

shall be multiplied by 26 the number representing 
the number of working days in a month , Similarly , 
the increment per month should be calculated and 
the two sums totalled to arrive at total monthly 
wagos of the different categories of workmen " . He 
then fixed the dearness allowance payable for each 
category of employees" . 


10 . In the first place as I have earlier pointed out it is 
not even the stand of the management that the workers are 
paid minimum wages or above the minimum wages under 
the Minimum Wages Act . Secondly , in the earlier Award 
fixing the wages in I. D . 39 of 1966 the monthly rate of wages 
had not been first determined and thercufter dividing the same 
by 26 to fix daily wages . Hence , no reliance whatsoever can 
be placed on Rule 23 of the Minimum Wages Central Rule 
1950 . Industrial Law in our country has clearly come to a 
stage to take the vicw that where two judicial choices Are 
plausible the construction in conformity with the social, 
philosophy of Article 44 of our Constitution has to be pre 
ferred . In the light of these circumstances I am unable to 
accept the contention of the Management that the workmen 
of the Management will not be entitled to payment of wages 
for weekly day off rest. It should also be remembered that 
the Management does not dispute that every workmen is en 
titled to a duy off rest after six days of work . The financial 
burden on the Management consequent on the demands under 
the reference has also to be considercd . The Salem district 
Magnesite Labour Union in paragraph 9 of the claims state 
ment had mentioned that the Management have the capacity 
to meet this demand and the monthly commitment in this 
regard will not be more than Rs. 10 , 000 . In paragraph 9 of 
the Counter statement, the Respondent Company " dcnic the 
allegations that the financial burden on the management, 18 
a result of the demand under reference , is only small and 
the management capable of meeting the game" . But signiAcan 
tly the Management has not challenged the claim of the union 
that the additional commitment would ongy be in the region 
of Rs. 10 ,000 per month . No doubt the Respondent Manage 
ment had stated that in the event of payment of wages for 
the rest separately tho liability of the Respondent Company 
will increase not only the payment of the wage bill but also 
in other respects as well as Provident Fund , Bonus , Leave 
pay etc . But oven thon the Respondent Management did not 
contond that it can 11 afford to bear this additional commit 
ment if ordered . It must be mentioned that tho learned Counsel 
for the Respondent Management did not address any argument 
on the financial burden that may be cast on the Respondent 
management in the cvent of the payment of wages for the 
rest day being allowed . The Respondent management which 
is a reputed Company of long record has capacitated to bear 
this additional burden . Magnesite in Salem district is being 
mined by three companies namely the Respondent manage 
ment Dalmia magnesite Corporation and Bum and Company 
Limited and Salem Magnesite Private Limited . It transpires 
in evidence that each of these three companies is earning same 
percentage of profits on about the same investment. Actually 
the Respondent Corporation is said to be bigger in the matter 
of investments and profits than Salem Magnesite Private Limi 
ted. The attendanco bonus scheme was in vogue in Burn and 
Company . Although Attendance bonus schemo may not be 
strictly equated to a weekly regt day wage , in effect the afore 
said company was also making payment of wages for the 
rent day . Therefore , it is apparent that in the samc region 
two of the magnesito companies with no better profits than 
the Respondent management are literally paying rest day wages 
to their employees . Thoge facts can also be taken into consi 
deration on the basis of the region wise industry . That would 
also justify the ordering of the payment of the workers of 
the Respondent management off weekly rest day . Looked at 
from many point of view , a case is made out for payment 
of wages for rest days to the workers of the Respondent 
Management. This will be at the rate of their usual daily 
wage inclusive of Dearness Allowance and other allowances. 


7 . On bebalf of the management, the learned counsel Thiru 
Govindaswaminathan pointed out that in the aforesaid award 
the monthly wages bad been determined by multiplying daily 
wages by 26 And therefore the wages paid for a month must 

hell to include rest days also and 25 such the workmen 
will not be entitled to any separate wages for rest days as 
claimed by them . It must be remembered that in the award 
J. D . 39 of 1966 the total wages earned by the worker for 
cach month had been arrived at. In 1961 II L . L . ) , page 632 
( Between Workmen of Bombay Port Trust and Trustees of 
the Port of Bombay ) the Supreme Court considered the issue 
about wages for the weekly rest day . In that case also the 
Management raised an identical contention like this that the 
workmen wero paid monthly wages , wages constructively for 
the offs also . This view has been repelled by the Supreme 
Court at page 637 and this is how they had ended this finding 
" By no stretch of imagination can payment for six days be 
cquated for payment for seven days " . 


8 . On behalf of the workmen reliance is placed on the 
Jutest decision of the Supreme Court reported in 1978 
I L . L . J . page 29 (Betwcen B . Shah and Labour Court , Colm 
batore and others ) where the question was raised as to how 
to compute maternity benefit for a female worker engaged on 
a daily wage basis : Whether a wageless Sunday should be 
included or should it be excluded ? Their lordships held a 
" week " has to be taken to signify a cpcle of seven days 
including Sunday . The benefit conferred must be extended 
to Sundays also . Although that decision might not directly 
be applied to the facts of the present case , it will throw 
considerable light as how a week should be considered in 
cluding a Sunday a rest day .

Rule 23 of Minimum Wages Central Rule 1950 which reads 
as follows :

3 . The several circumstances preceded the aforesaid settle

4 )- per metric tonnes mit and there will be proportionate

month and will be based on collective output per shift. There 
fore it is clear from the aforesaid terms that the workmen 
have to necessarily turn out five metric tonnes load per hoad 
per shift and if per chance they had not reached that figure 
they will be pald only proportinoatcly , 


5. Learned counsel for the Management has clearly point 
out that he has produced these documents only ag samples to 
show how the load is weighed at Cement Weighbridge. Little 
purpose will be served by producing tho entire documents . 
But from the above unimpcachable materials it can be seen , 
that the weighment of Lime stoncs supplied by the workwen 
had tho approval of the authority of the Revenue Department 
as well as the Excise Department of tho Central Governmont. 
who were respectively collecting royalty and exciso duty . All 
these registers would also indicate that these registers had 
becn checked by the officials of the Revenue and Excise De 
partment. It is true that at some stage the workmen refused 
to subscribe his signature to the weighment made of tho 
India Cements weighbridge . However , the Respondente 
Management had no other option but to continuo to do the 
weighment even in the absence of the workmen , Tho chiet 
complaint of the Union is that weighment was not donc in 
the presence of the workmen and thereforo irrespectivo of 
thre out turn given by them , the workmen had to be paid in 
full . I am afraid this submission cangot be accepted , Learned 
counsel for the Union would also point out that the weigh 
ment in the absence of the work would be violation of rulon 
relating to Payment of Wages Mines Rules, 1956 . If any 
proceedings are launched against tho Rospondent-Management 
for failure to observe the rules they will have to face tho 
consequences but the hard fact remaing that the actual load 
turned out by the workmen had been recorded at India 
Cemonts Weighbridge and accepted by the Management. It 
is not denied that welghment slips obtained from the India 
Cements was pastod in the Notice Board of the Respondent

of the Respondadia

4 . Rogarunig paid festiyul boliduys I do not think any

in fact apart from the affidavit no other evidence has been 
led to show by the workmen that there is any agreement 
between the workm¢n Md the workers uoion in this behalf,

Covered . The workmen have also failed to show figures 
of their output and production or to bring out the financial 
burden or expenditure which would be involved i å prac 
tice to grant festival holidays is introduced . While agreeing 
that there is nood for standardisation of service conditions 
includin those relating to grant of festival holidays . I am 
afraid timo is not yet ripe to grant festival holidays except 
where there are agreements between the parties . The work 
men have not cgtablished that thero are any standing orders 
Tanting any festival holidays. The model standing orders 
tinder The Industrial Employment ( Standing Orders ) Act, 
1946 apparently are not applicable to these workmen , Kecp

Citse has been made out for grant of y puid festival holi

that the workmen are not entitled to any paid holidays over 
and above these three national holidays awarded above.

9 . With regard to the last charge said to be proved it

" A delay of about 14 years must be considered fatal 

from the point of view of affording reasonable 
opportunity to the coriployee concerned to show cause 
against the charge levelled against him . It would 
bo asking for the impossible to expect the employee 
concerned to explain satisfactorily the leason which 
occasioned the delay in reporting for duty . If the 
chargo or accusation had been levelled very soon 
after the lapse , the employee concerned could have 
rendered an appropriate cxplanation regardless of 
whether it was or was not cons . dered satisfactory 
by the competent authority . Not having done so 
for more than 14 years after the occuirence , the 
employce cannot be penalisod for not being able to 
show cause to the satisfaction of the diciplinary 
Authority . Under the circumstances, 
in initiating proceedings must be held to constitute 
A denial of reasonable opportunity to defend him 
self for one cannot reasonably expect an employee 
to have a computer- like memory or to maintain a 
day - to - day diary in which very small matter is 
meticulously recorded in anticipation of future 
eventualities of which he cannot have a prevision 
Nor can be be expected to adduce evidence to establish 
his innocence for after inordinate delay he would 
not recall the identity of the witnesses who could 
support him . Delay by itself, therefore , would 
constitute denial of rcgnon ble opportunity to show 
cause . This would amount to violation of principles 
of natural justice and the impugned Order of 

removal from service must be struck down" .

isques :

( 2 ) The facts leading up to the present dispute are not it 
controversy . The Respondent is the Management of Chettired 
Cement Corporation Limited , Puliyur Cement Factory Pust , 
Karur Tuluk , Tiruchirapalli District. The cement factory of 
the Regpondent Corporation is located at Puliyur , Karur Taluk , 
Trichy District, whereas lime-stone mines are situuled 20 miles 
away from the factory at Alambadi, Vadachandur Tuluk , 
Madurai District . There are about 600 workers employed by 
the Management in tho cement factory and about 150) worhes 
in the lime stone mines . Tho present reference relates only to 
the workmen employed in Alambadi limestone mines by the 
Respondent-Management. The workmen employed by the Res 
pondent-Managenient in Alambadi limo- tone mines hic IC 
presented by 4 Unions, viz . , Chattinad Cement Corporation 
Uzhaippalar Sangam , Chettinad Cement Quarry Workery 
Union , Chettinad Cement Corporation Anna Thozhilalar Union 
and Cement Workers Progressive Union . The four Unions 
have filed separato claim statements but they are agreed in 
this (1 ) , for the fitment of the various workers is proper 
categories and ( ii ) for claiming night shift allowance 
and washing allowance . Since the claims of all workmen re 
presented by the four Unions are substantially identical the 
Respondent-Managemont has filed a common counter state 
ment contending that tho existing fitment of workmen mado 
by tho Respondent- Corporation is justified and needs no 
rovision and that the workmen employed by the Respondent 
Management would not be entitled to the night shift and 
washing allowance . The Managoment thus contonded that 
tho claim with reference to issue No. 2 must be rejected 
while so far as issue No . 1 is concerned a qualified technical 
Assessor may be appointed to submit a report in respect of 
the various categories of workers and that he may also be 
directed to suggest the gradation of those categories . Sub 
sequently , the Management feld Miscellaneous Application 
No. 19 , of 1971 under section 11 clauso 5 of the Industrial 
Disputes Act, 1947 to appoint a third party independent 
Agossor with technical qualification to visit the quarries and 
gubmit a detailed report regarding the categorisation and 
gradation of various types of workmen employed in the 
quarries . This application was resisted by the Unions con 
tending that the appointment of an Assessor is not necessary 
and this is only an attempt to prolong the issue. On 17 - 2 - 1977. 
my learned predecessor passed an order appoloting an Asses 
NOT to go into the question and submit the report. From 
the order, it is seen that the Management has undertaken to 
bear the cost of the Assessor and the Tribunal has also passed 
an order that the remineration of tho Assessor will be fixed 
later and it will be paid by the Management. Consequently 
my lcarned predecessor appointed the Madras Productivity 
Council, Madras as An Assessor to make a study of the 
working in the quarries and submit report relating to cate 
gorisation and gradation of various workmen with liberty 
for both parties to submit memo of instructions to Asgessor, 
Tho Madras Productivity Council undertook the job evalu 
ation as directed by this Tubunal of Alambadi Mines of 
Chettinad Cement Corporation Limited . Puliyor. Ex , M - 1 is 
the Job Evaluation Report submitted by tho Madras Producti 
vity Council . Even from this Report Ex . M - 1 it can be 
gathered that firstly It was decidod that Management should 


(3 ) At this stage another significant development took place . 
While the matter was pending before this Tribunal the work . 
men of the cement industry represented by tho Indian Na 
tional Cement and Allied Workers Federation , Bombay - 4 
raised demands against the einployers of the Cement indus 
try represented by Cement Manufacturers Association , Bom 
bay and those demanda were referred to the National Arbi 
tration Board consisting of Shri G , Ramanujam and Shri R . P . 
Nevatia for arbitration by Government of India by its order 
No. L - 29013 / 2 /77- D - III- 8 dated 28th November, 1977 and 
No . L - 29013 / 2 /77 - D - III - 8 , dated 9th January , 1978. The ar 
bitration board rendered its Award on 26th September 1978 . 
Ex. M - 6 is the printed copy of that Award . Annexure VI of 
Ex , M - 6 dealt with occupational nomenclature with job des 
criptions and their classification into grades of various work 
men employed in cement factories and quarries on a national 
basis. The question of payment of night shift allowance and 
the washing allowance had also been decided by thc said 
Award of Arbitration Board _ vide paragraphs 172 to 182 
and 184 The Award came into force with effect from 1 - 10 
1978 , That being so on 9 - 12 - 1978 , tho Respondent-Manage 
ment and the workmen employed by the Respondent -Manage 
ment had agreed before this Tribunal to abide by the terms 
of award Ex . M - 6 and to work out tho consequent details 
la discussions between the Management and the Unions and 
such discussions went on for some time. Both the parties 
held scries of bilateral discussions on the question of imple 
mentation of tho award relating to the workmen employed in 
limestone mines . Ex . M - 5 are the terms of settlement under 
Section 18 ( 1 ) of the Industrial Disputes Act entered into 
between the Management and the workmen represented by two 
Unions , viz ., Chettinad Cement Quay Workers Union and 
Chettinad Cement Corporation Anna Thozhilalar Union out 
of four Unions on 13 - 10 - 1979 , Two Unions which did not 
subscribe to the settlement under Ex. M - 5 bad fled their ob 
jections before this Tribural. shall now advert to the ob 
jections raised by them 


( 4 ) In the first place these two Unions , viz ., Chettinad 
Cement Corporation Uzhaippalar Sangam (Union No . 1) and 
Coment Workers Progressive Union ( Union No. 4 ) contend 
that in as much as they did not agree to the Settlement under 
Ex, M - 5 , the terms of those settlements would not bind their 
workmen , Needless to point out that in as much 49 the Settlo 
ment under Ex . M - S , is only one under Section 18 ( 1 ) it will 
necessai ily in law bind only the parties thereto . Both these 
Unions faintly contended that in 9 much as Union No. 4 
alone had originally raised the demands roşulting in the present 
reference any settlement without its consent must be held to 
be bad in law . But this position has been repolled by a Divi 
sion Bench of our High Court in Writ Petition No. 2742 / 76 
dated 1 - 2 - 1979 reported in a matter arises from this Tribunal 
in I. D . No. 41 / 1975. Thereforo after this decision of the Divi . 
sion Bench such a contention cannot be countenanced , Tho 
claim of Union No. 4 is that the Union , which are parties

alleged signatures opposing tho Settlement are enclosed in

by Union No, 4 which is inarked as Ex . W - 12 . Byt gicat re 
liance cannot be placed on this socalled Annexure of work 
men objecting to the Settlement for the following reasons . For 
their part, the two Unions who are parties to the Settlement 
under Ex . M - 5 have filed a list of workmen who had agreed 
to abide by this Settlement and the two lists are together 
marked as Ex, M - 8 . It may be noted that Ex, M -8 had been

under Ex . M - 5 . Therufore even on a consideration of the 
totality of the materials now placed there is room to say that 
the majority of workmen support the settlement entered into 
under Ex . M - 5 . Although if majority of workmen support 
a settlement that can be taken into consideration as one 
factor to support the fairness and justness of the terms of 
the settlement the converse case , viz ., that merely because a 
minority of the workmen support the terms of the settlement 
it does not necessarily follow that the termsof settlement are 
not fair , unjust or unequitable . In this context one significant 
circumstance should also be bome in mind . Union No. 4 
i e , Cement Workers Progressive Union which claims to be 
the majority Union claiming i work force of 197 49 evident 
from Ex . W - 12 had participated in the discussions hold with 
the Management for 15 bearings . As a result of detailed 
discussions on 18 - 9 - 1979 three Unions including Chettinad 
Cement Corporation Qlurry Workers Progressive Union und 
approved the draft terms of settlement as will be bosne out 
by Ex . M -7 . It is not as though the Union reprenentative had 
simply affixed his signature in Ex . M - 7 without agrecing to 
the terms of the settlement. Further moro , even in the objec + 
tion filed by the fourth Union there is no whisper that the 
Union had consented t :) the terms of settlement under any 
inisapprehension . In the absence of any such claim , cven In 
the objection it is perfectly clear that even the majority 
Unioo represented by the fourth Union had in fact Agreed 
to the terms of the settlement as mentioned in Ex . M - 7 . 
The terms mentioned in Ex . M -7 are translated into details 
in the settlement Ex . M - 5 . Therefore three Unions includ 
ing fourth Union had infact consented to the terms of settle 
ment entered into under Ex . M - 7 and Ex , M - S . In that 
view Union No. 4 is also bound by the terms of settlement 
entered into under Ex. M -7 and M - 5 . In as much as on the 
facts appearing in this dispute substantial majority of the 
workmen have supported the torms of settlement under Exs. 
M - 7 and M - S it is not ripreasonable to hold that the terms of 
settlement entered into under Ex . M -5 are just , fair and 
equtable . 


of the Award of Arbitration Board for Coment Industry as 
scop from clause 207 of Ex, M -6 must have to be implemented . 
In that view when the greater majority of the workmen had 
agrecd to the terms o : settlement Ex . M - 5 it must follo ? 
that the terms must be held to be fair and just. At this 
stage it will be pertinent for me to point out that the terms 
worked out in the settlement Ex . M - 5 are in perfect con 
sonance with the guidelines laid down in the Award of Arbi 
trution Board for Cement Industry 1979 Ex M - 6 , Thoicfore 
in the larger interest of industrial peace and for the sake of 
uniformity in service conditions the terms of the Award of 
Arbitration Board for Cement Industry 1978 had to be imple 
mented . In 1973 — 11 — , L . J — Page 144 ( The workmen of 
Government Silk Weaving Factory , Myrort vs . The Presiding 
Oflicer, Industrial Tribunal, Bangalore and others ) , the 
Supreme Court held that where a Tribunal has rocorded a 
finding to the eflect that the settlement was bewoon a sub 
stantial number of workmen on the one hand and the manage 
ment of the pending disputes, that it was a genuine settlo 
ment and the terms of the settlement were very fair and just . 
In the light of the aforesaid decision of the Supreme Court, 
tho present settlement Ex . M - 5 based on the Award of Arbi 
tration Board for Cement Industry 1978 Ex . M - 6 must be 
held to be very fair and just . In 1979 (54 ) F . J . R . - Page 249 
( Herbertsons Limited vs . their workmen and others ) the 
Supreme Court has laid down guidelines as to when a given 
settlement can be held to be just and fair , The Supreme 
Court points out that as far as the parties are concerned there 
will always be uncertainty with regard to the ultimate result 
of the litigation in Court proceedings. In the instant case , 
the litigation thus far had a checkered carcor . The reference 
to this Court was made by the Government as early on 27th 
February , 1976 . Thereafter for obtaining the Assessor s 
report, considered time has been spent. The Assessor s Report 
is marked as Ex . M - 1 . All the four Unions have fllod obiec 
tions to the Assessor s Report . Thereafter all the Union , and 
the Management had examined witnesses on their behalf, At 
that juncture the Award of Arbitration Board for Cement 
Industry was rendered . Whereupon all the partics including 
the Management and the four Unions had agreed to work 
out the remedies pursuant to the Award of Arbitration Board 

ur Cement Industry 1978 Ex. M -6 . The four Unlong started 
discussions with the Management for about 3 or 4 months, 
Eventually , three Unions representing the majority of work 
men and agreed to the terms of Ex M - 7 wbich wera worked 
out in the Settlement Ex . M - 5 . Now two Unions would like 
to dig - associate themselves from the settlement. Already liti 
gations were pending for about 4 years and there will be no 
knowing as to what would be the ultimate fate of the dispute . 
It should also be redembered that the Respondent 
Management is not big concem but is only 
employed about 173 workmen in the limestono mines . Taking 
into consideration tho job evaluation report Ex . M - 1 and the 
Award of Arbitration Board for Comcat Industry . Ex . M - 6 
and the never ending litigations, it is not improbable that on 
a mature consideration and calm reflection , the workomen had 
decided to como to terme under Ex . M - 5 . As the Supremo 
Court has poipted out that it is not possible to scan 4 settle 
ment in bite and pieces and hold somo parts good and accept 
ablc and others had . It should also be borne in mind that 
thero is absolutely no allegation of mala fides or fraud or 
even corruption or other inducements for the workmen to 
have agreed to terms of settlement Ex , M - S . The terms of 
settlement must be held to be gepuine. On an over all exa 
mination of the materials oral, documeutary and the Assessor s 
Report Ex . M - 1 and the Award of Arbitration Board for 
Cement Industry Ex . M - 6 , it must be held that all the cate

By Order No. L - 19012 / 17 / 76 - D . III B dated 26th Novom 
ber , 1976 , the Government of India , Ministry of Labour, 
referred an industrial dispute oxisting between the manage 
nent of Bhanora Colliery and Shri Samrathi Yadav, to this 
Tribunal, for adjudication . The schedule to the order of re 
ference reads as : 


" Whether the management of Bhanora Colliery of Bha 

nora Sub - Arca of Eastern Coalficlds Ltd ., P . O . 
Charanpur, Dist , Burdwan was justified in denying 
employment to Shri Samrathi Yaday with effect from 
4 - 10 - 1974 ? If not, what relief the workman iten 
titled to ]" 


9 . From January 1974 declaration forms were obtained from 
casual wagon loaders to enable the employer to preparo 
pool of casual wagon londers. On 3rd August, 1974 one porn 
son said to be Samrathi Yadav , son of Johar Yadav got him 
self ensoled after filling up the declaration form , Soon thero 
after he was detectod to be not the concerned Samrathi 
Yadav who had worked carlier and soon after the detection 
was made he was stopped from working from 4th Oct. 1974 . 
As it was a clear case of in . personation the employer had to 
stop him from working in the colliery . Moreover , & casual 
workman whoxo service , begin in the morning and ends in 
the evening, if at all work can be provided for him , had no 
right as guch to work as a casual workor . There can not 
therefore be any valid risputo regarding employment or non 
employroent of Auch a person whose very nature of work 
is transient in character and is otherwise dependant upon a 
number of other factors . 


2 . The case ag pleaded on behalf of Samrathi Yadav , tho 
concerned workman , is that he was employed by Equitable 
Coal Company Ltd ., che erstwhile owners of Bhanurm col 
liery as a wagon loading mazdoor. He had been working 
for about 3 or 4 years when the management of the colliery 
was taken over by the Central Government. On nationalisa 
tion of the colliery , he was employed as a wagon loader 
under Coal Mincs Authority Lad . And thercafter under Eaj 
tern Coalfields Limited , 


10 . In answer to "he written statement Aled on behalf 
of Sri Yadav , the management stated that the job of wagon 
loading may be a perennial job but the deployment of wagon 
loadery depends entirely upon supply of wagons which is 
erratic , and for days there may not be any placement at all 
when necessarily such casual wagon londors cannot be em 
ployed . The management denied that the concerned work 
man was working for more than 24 days in a month , Ho la 
not entitled to any relief. 


3 . In paragraph 1 of the writton statement Aled on behalt 
of the concerned workman it is stated that though the work 
was of permanent nature and ho had been working for more 
than 24 days in a month , continually and regularly , the pri 
vate management in order to deprive him and others of their 
legitimate dues did not diako them permanent and the con 
cerned workman was designated as casual wagun londer 
though he performed the job of a permanent nature . 


11 . At the hearing of the roterenco , the learned advocate 
did not press the objection as to the maintainability of the 
reference on the grolind that no demand had been made 
and tho Union had no lccire standi to espouse the cause of 
the concerned workman . 


4 . The concerned workman was given bonus card and his 
name was also entered in the B form register. All of . sud 
den , he was stopped from working with effect from 4th Octo 
ber , 1974 on the ground that he was got the real Sanirathi 
Yadav. The denial of employment to the concerneil work 
man by the managemert is illogal and he is entitled to he 
re - employed with effect from 4th October, 1974 wlth all 
back wages and other benefits which he should have carncd
